<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) को पहुँचा के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अराफ़ात तमाम ठहरने की जगह है मगर बत्न-ए-अराफ़ा में न ठहरो, और मुज़दलिफ़ा तमाम ठहरने की जगह है मगर बत्न-ए-मुहस्सर में न ठहरो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 873]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/873" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک کو پہنچا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا عرفات تمام ٹھہرنے کی جگہ ہے مگر بطن عرفہ میں نہ ٹھہرو اور مزدلفہ تمام ٹھہرنے کی جگہ ہے مگر بطن محسر میں نہ ٹھہرو۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 873]</p>

## Narration 871

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A874)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 874]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/874" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 874</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   874 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ اعْلَمُوا أَنَّ عَرَفَةَ كُلَّهَا مَوْقِفٌ إِلاَّ بَطْنَ عُرَنَةَ وَأَنَّ الْمُزْدَلِفَةَ كُلَّهَا مَوْقِفٌ إِلاَّ بَطْنَ مُحَسِّرٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ قَالَ اللَّهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏فَلاَ رَفَثَ وَلاَ فُسُوقَ وَلاَ جِدَالَ فِي الْحَجِّ ‏}‏ قَالَ فَالرَّفَثُ إِصَابَةُ النِّسَاءِ وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ قَالَ اللَّهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏أُحِلَّ لَكُمْ لَيْلَةَ الصِّيَامِ الرَّفَثُ إِلَى نِسَائِكُمْ ‏}‏ قَالَ وَالْفُسُوقُ الذَّبْحُ لِلأَنْصَابِ وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ قَالَ اللَّهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏أَوْ فِسْقًا أُهِلَّ لِغَيْرِ اللَّهِ بِهِ‏}‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَالْجِدَالُ فِي الْحَجِّ أَنَّ قُرَيْشًا كَانَتْ تَقِفُ عِنْدَ الْمَشْعَرِ الْحَرَامِ بِالْمُزْدَلِفَةِ بِقُزَحَ وَكَانَتِ الْعَرَبُ وَغَيْرُهُمْ يَقِفُونَ بِعَرَفَةَ فَكَانُوا يَتَجَادَلُونَ يَقُولُ هَؤُلاَءِ نَحْنُ أَصْوَبُ وَيَقُولُ هَؤُلاَءِ نَحْنُ أَصْوَبُ فَقَالَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى وَ ‏{‏لِكُلِّ أُمَّةٍ جَعَلْنَا مَنْسَكًا هُمْ نَاسِكُوهُ فَلاَ يُنَازِعُنَّكَ فِي الأَمْرِ وَادْعُ إِلَى رَبِّكَ إِنَّكَ لَعَلَى هُدًى مُسْتَقِيمٍ‏}‏ فَهَذَا الْجِدَالُ فِيمَا نُرَى وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ وَقَدْ سَمِعْتُ ذَلِكَ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/874" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn az-Zubayr (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa that Abdullah ibn az-Zubayr used to say, &quot;Know that the whole of Arafa is a standing-place except for the middle of Urana, and that the wholeof Muzdalifa is a standing-place except for the middle of Muhassir.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 874]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/874" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 874</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   874 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;Allah, the Blessed and Exalted says, &#x27;There is to be no rafath, no fusuq and no jidal during the hajj.&#x27; &quot; (Sura 2 ayat 197). He added, &quot;Rafath is sexual relations with women, and Allah knows best. Allah, the Blessed and Exalted says, &#x27;Rafath with your women is permitted to you on the night of the fast.&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 197). Fusuq are sacrifices made to idols, and Allah knows best. Allah, the Blessed and Exalted, says, &#x27;Or a fisq offered up to other than Allah.&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 197) Jidal (arguing) during the hajj refers to when the Quraysh used to stand near the mashar al-haram at Quzah in Muzdalifa, while the Arabs and others would stand at Arafa, and they would argue about who was the more correct. Allah, the Blessed and Exalted, says, &#x27;And we appointed a method of sacrifice for every nation, which they followed, so let them not dispute with you about the matter, and call to your Lord. Surely you are on a straight guidance.&#x27; (Sura 22 ayat 67) This is what jidal refers to in our opinion, and Allah knows best. This I have heard from the people of knowledge</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/874" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन ज़ुबैर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे: जान लो तुम के अराफ़ात सारा ठहरने की जगह है मगर बत्न-ए-मुहस्सर। कहा मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) ने, फ़रमाया अल्लाह तआला ने: &#x27;फ़ला रफ़सा वला फ़ुसूक़ा वला जिदाला फ़िल हज&#x27; (न रफ़स है, न फ़िस्क है, न झगड़ा है हज में)। तो रफ़स के मआनी जमा&#x27; के हैं और अल्लाह ख़ूब जानता है, फ़रमाया है &#x27;उहिल्ला लकुम लैलातस-सियामिल रफ़सु इला निसा-इकुम&#x27; (हलाल है तुम्हारे लिए रोज़ों की रात में जमा&#x27; अपनी औरतों से), यहाँ पर रफ़स से जमा&#x27; मुराद है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/874" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن زبیر کہتے تھے جانو تم کہ عرفات سارا ٹھہرنے کی جگہ ہے مگر بطن محسر ۔ کہا مالک نے فرمایا اللہ تعالیٰ نے فلا رفث ولا فسوق ولاجدال فی الحج، نہ رفث ہے نہ فسق ہے نہ جھگڑا ہے حج میں، تو رفث کے معنی جماع کے ہیں اور اللہ خوب جانتا ہے فرمایا ہے احل لکم لیلة الصیام الرفث الی نساءکم حلال ہے تمہارے لئے روزوں کی رات میں جماع اپنی عورتوں سے یہاں پر رفث سے جماع مراد ہے۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Zubair (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte thay: Jaan lo tum ke Arafat sara theherne ki jagah hai magar batn-e-Muhassar. Kaha Malik (Rehmatullah Alaih) ne, farmaya Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne: &#x27;Fala rafatha wala fusooqa wala jidala fil hajj&#x27; (na rafath hai, na fisq hai, na jhagda hai hajj mein). Toh rafath ke ma&#x27;ani jama&#x27; ke hain aur Allah khoob jaanta hai, farmaya hai &#x27;Uhilla lakum lailatas-siyamil rafathu ila nisa&#x27;ikum&#x27; (halal hai tumhare liye rozon ki raat mein jama&#x27; apni aurton se), yahan par rafath se jama&#x27; murad hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/874" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 872

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A875)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَقُولُ مَنْ لَمْ يَقِفْ بِعَرَفَةَ مِنْ لَيْلَةِ الْمُزْدَلِفَةِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَطْلُعَ الْفَجْرُ فَقَدْ فَاتَهُ الْحَجُّ وَمَنْ وَقَفَ بِعَرَفَةَ مِنْ لَيْلَةِ الْمُزْدَلِفَةِ مِنْ قَبْلِ أَنْ يَطْلُعَ الْفَجْرُ فَقَدْ أَدْرَكَ الْحَجَّ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar used to say, &quot;Someone who does not stand at Arafa on the night of Muzdalifa before the dawn breaks has missed the hajj, and someone who stands at Arafa on the night of Muzdalifa before the dawn breaks has caught the hajj</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 875]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/875" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 875</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   875 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik was asked about whether a man could stand at Arafa, or at Muzdalifa, or stone the Jamras, or do say between Safa and Marwa if he was not in wudu, and he said, &quot;Every practice in the hajj that a menstruating woman can take part in can be taken part in by a man who is not in wudu and there is nothing due from him for that. However, it is better for him to be in wudu for all those things, and he should not make a general practice of it.&quot; Malik was asked whether a man who was riding should get down to do the standing at Arafa or if he could stand while mounted, and he said, &quot;He can stand while mounted, unless he or his riding beast have an illness, in which case Allah is the one who most often accepts an excuse.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/875" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 875]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/875" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 875</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   875 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक से पूछा गया के क्या कोई आदमी अराफात में खड़ा हो सकता है, या मुज़दलिफ़ा में, या जमरात पर कंकरियां मार सकता है, या सफा ओ मरवा के दरमियान सई कर सकता है अगर वो बा-वुज़ू न हो, तो उन्होंने कहा, हज्ज का हर वो अमल जिसमें हैज़ वाली औरत हिस्सा ले सकती है, उसमें वो मर्द भी हिस्सा ले सकता है जो बा-वुज़ू न हो और उसपर उसके लिए कुछ वाजिब नहीं है। ताहम, उसके लिए बेहतर है के वो इन तमाम कामों के लिए बा-वुज़ू रहे, और उसे इसे मामूल नहीं बनाना चाहिए। इमाम मालिक से पूछा गया के क्या सवार आदमी को अराफात में वुकूफ़ के लिए उतरना चाहिए या वो सवारी पर ही खड़ा हो सकता है, तो उन्होंने कहा, वो सवारी पर ही खड़ा हो सकता है, सिवाए इसके के उसे या उसकी सवारी को कोई बीमारी हो, ऐसी सूरत में अल्लाह ही है जो सब से ज़्यादा उज़्र क़बूल करता है। यह्या ने मुझसे इमाम मालिक से और उन्होंने नाफ़ि से रिवायत की के अब्दुल्लाह इब्न उमर (रज़ि.) कहा करते थे, जो शख़्स मुज़दलिफ़ा की रात तुलू-ए-फ़ज्र से पहले अराफात में नहीं खड़ा होता, उसका हज्ज फ़ौत हो गया, और जो शख़्स मुज़दलिफ़ा की रात तुलू-ए-फ़ज्र से पहले अराफात में खड़ा होता है, उसने हज्ज पा लिया।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/875" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک سے پوچھا گیا کہ کیا کوئی آدمی عرفات میں کھڑا ہو سکتا ہے، یا مزدلفہ میں، یا جمرات پر کنکریاں مار سکتا ہے، یا صفا و مروہ کے درمیان سعی کر سکتا ہے اگر وہ باوضو نہ ہو، تو انہوں نے کہا، حج کا ہر وہ عمل جس میں حیض والی عورت حصہ لے سکتی ہے، اس میں وہ مرد بھی حصہ لے سکتا ہے جو باوضو نہ ہو اور اس پر اس کے لیے کچھ واجب نہیں ہے۔ تاہم، اس کے لیے بہتر ہے کہ وہ ان تمام کاموں کے لیے باوضو رہے، اور اسے اسے معمول نہیں بنانا چاہیے۔ امام مالک سے پوچھا گیا کہ کیا سوار آدمی کو عرفات میں وقوف کے لیے اترنا چاہیے یا وہ سواری پر ہی کھڑا ہو سکتا ہے، تو انہوں نے کہا، وہ سواری پر ہی کھڑا ہو سکتا ہے، سوائے اس کے کہ اسے یا اس کی سواری کو کوئی بیماری ہو، ایسی صورت میں اللہ ہی ہے جو سب سے زیادہ عذر قبول کرتا ہے۔ یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے اور انہوں نے نافع سے روایت کی کہ عبداللہ بن عمر (رضی اللہ عنہ) کہا کرتے تھے، جو شخص مزدلفہ کی رات طلوعِ فجر سے پہلے عرفات میں نہیں کھڑا ہوتا، اس کا حج فوت ہو گیا، اور جو شخص مزدلفہ کی رات طلوعِ فجر سے پہلے عرفات میں کھڑا ہوتا ہے، اس نے حج پا لیا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/875" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/875" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 875</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   875 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik se pucha gaya ke kya koi aadmi Arafat mein khara ho sakta hai, ya Muzdalifa mein, ya Jamrat par kankariyan maar sakta hai, ya Safa o Marwa ke darmiyan sa&#x27;i kar sakta hai agar wo ba-wuzu na ho, to unhone kaha, Hajj ka har wo amal jismein haiz wali aurat hissa le sakti hai, usmein wo mard bhi hissa le sakta hai jo ba-wuzu na ho aur uspar uske liye kuch wajib nahi hai. Taham, uske liye behtar hai ke wo in tamam kamon ke liye ba-wuzu rahe, aur use ise mamool nahi banana chahiye. Imam Malik se pucha gaya ke kya sawar aadmi ko Arafat mein wuquf ke liye utarna chahiye ya wo sawari par hi khara ho sakta hai, to unhone kaha, wo sawari par hi khara ho sakta hai, siwaye iske ke use ya uski sawari ko koi bimari ho, aisi surat mein Allah hi hai jo sab se zyada uzr qabool karta hai. Yahya ne mujhse Imam Malik se aur unhone Nafi se riwayat ki ke Abdullah ibn Umar (RA) kaha karte the, jo shakhs Muzdalifa ki raat tulu-e-fajr se pehle Arafat mein nahi khara hota, uska Hajj faut ho gaya, aur jo shakhs Muzdalifa ki raat tulu-e-fajr se pehle Arafat mein khara hota hai, usne Hajj pa liya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/875" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 873

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A876)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 876]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 876</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   876 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ مَنْ أَدْرَكَهُ الْفَجْرُ مِنْ لَيْلَةِ الْمُزْدَلِفَةِ وَلَمْ يَقِفْ بِعَرَفَةَ فَقَدْ فَاتَهُ الْحَجُّ وَمَنْ وَقَفَ بِعَرَفَةَ مِنْ لَيْلَةِ الْمُزْدَلِفَةِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَطْلُعَ الْفَجْرُ فَقَدْ أَدْرَكَ الْحَجَّ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْعَبْدِ يُعْتَقُ فِي الْمَوْقِفِ بِعَرَفَةَ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ لاَ يَجْزِي عَنْهُ مِنْ حَجَّةِ الإِسْلاَمِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ لَمْ يُحْرِمْ فَيُحْرِمُ بَعْدَ أَنْ يُعْتَقَ ثُمَّ يَقِفُ بِعَرَفَةَ مِنْ تِلْكَ اللَّيْلَةِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَطْلُعَ الْفَجْرُ فَإِنْ فَعَلَ ذَلِكَ أَجْزَأَ عَنْهُ وَإِنْ لَمْ يُحْرِمْ حَتَّى طَلَعَ الْفَجْرُ كَانَ بِمَنْزِلَةِ مَنْ فَاتَهُ الْحَجُّ إِذَا لَمْ يُدْرِكِ الْوُقُوفَ بِعَرَفَةَ قَبْلَ طُلُوعِ الْفَجْرِ مِنْ لَيْلَةِ الْمُزْدَلِفَةِ وَيَكُونُ عَلَى الْعَبْدِ حَجَّةُ الإِسْلاَمِ يَقْضِيهَا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: attributed saying. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The father’s attributed statement is kept with its source wording, including the supplied punctuation. The subsequent compiler ruling remains separate.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa that his father said, &quot;Someone who does not stand at Arafa on the night of Muzdalifa before the dawn breaks has missed the hajj, and some one who stands at Arafa on the night of Muzdalifa before the dawn breaks has caught the hajj.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 876]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 876</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   876 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, about a slave freed during the wuquf at Arafa, &quot;His standing does not fulfil for him the hajj of Islam, except if he was not in ihram and then he went into ihram after he was freed and he stood at Arafa that same night before the dawn broke in which case that is enough for him. If, however, he did not go into ihram until after the dawn had broken, he is in the same position as someone who misses the hajj by not catching the standing at Arafa before the breaking of the dawn on the night of Muzdalifa, and he will have to do the hajj of Islam later</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 876]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 876</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   876 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझसे इमाम मालिक से और उन्होंने हिशाम इब्न उरवा से रिवायत की के उनके वालिद ने कहा, जो शख़्स मुज़दलिफ़ा की रात तुलू-ए-फ़ज्र से पहले अराफात में खड़ा नहीं होता, उसका हज्ज फ़ौत हो गया, और जो शख़्स मुज़दलिफ़ा की रात तुलू-ए-फ़ज्र से पहले अराफात में खड़ा होता है, उसने हज्ज पा लिया। इमाम मालिक ने अराफात में वुकूफ़ के दौरान आज़ाद होने वाले ग़ुलाम के बारे में कहा, उसका वुकूफ़ इस्लाम के हज्ज को पूरा नहीं करता, सिवाए इसके के वो एहराम में न था और फिर आज़ाद होने के बाद उसने एहराम बांधा और उसी रात तुलू-ए-फ़ज्र से पहले अराफात में खड़ा हुआ, ऐसी सूरत में ये उसके लिए काफ़ी है। अगर, ताहम, उसने तुलू-ए-फ़ज्र के बाद एहराम बांधा, तो वो उसी मक़ाम पर है जैसे वो शख़्स जिसका हज्ज मुज़दलिफ़ा की रात तुलू-ए-फ़ज्र से पहले अराफात में वुकूफ़ न कर सकने की वजह से फ़ौत हो गया, और उसे बाद में इस्लाम का हज्ज करना होगा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے اور انہوں نے ہشام بن عروہ سے روایت کی کہ ان کے والد نے کہا، جو شخص مزدلفہ کی رات طلوعِ فجر سے پہلے عرفات میں کھڑا نہیں ہوتا، اس کا حج فوت ہو گیا، اور جو شخص مزدلفہ کی رات طلوعِ فجر سے پہلے عرفات میں کھڑا ہوتا ہے، اس نے حج پا لیا۔ امام مالک نے عرفات میں وقوف کے دوران آزاد ہونے والے غلام کے بارے میں کہا، اس کا وقوف اسلام کے حج کو پورا نہیں کرتا، سوائے اس کے کہ وہ احرام میں نہ تھا اور پھر آزاد ہونے کے بعد اس نے احرام باندھا اور اسی رات طلوعِ فجر سے پہلے عرفات میں کھڑا ہوا، ایسی صورت میں یہ اس کے لیے کافی ہے۔ اگر، تاہم، اس نے طلوعِ فجر کے بعد احرام باندھا، تو وہ اسی مقام پر ہے جیسے وہ شخص جس کا حج مزدلفہ کی رات طلوعِ فجر سے پہلے عرفات میں وقوف نہ کر سکنے کی وجہ سے فوت ہو گیا، اور اسے بعد میں اسلام کا حج کرنا ہوگا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   876 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhse Imam Malik se aur unhone Hisham ibn Urwa se riwayat ki ke unke walid ne kaha, jo shakhs Muzdalifa ki raat tulu-e-fajr se pehle Arafat mein khara nahi hota, uska Hajj faut ho gaya, aur jo shakhs Muzdalifa ki raat tulu-e-fajr se pehle Arafat mein khara hota hai, usne Hajj pa liya. Imam Malik ne Arafat mein wuquf ke dauran azad hone wale ghulam ke bare mein kaha, uska wuquf Islam ke Hajj ko pura nahi karta, siwaye iske ke wo ihram mein na tha aur phir azad hone ke baad usne ihram bandha aur usi raat tulu-e-fajr se pehle Arafat mein khara hua, aisi surat mein ye uske liye kafi hai. Agar, taham, usne tulu-e-fajr ke baad ihram bandha, to wo usi maqam par hai jaise wo shakhs jiska Hajj Muzdalifa ki raat tulu-e-fajr se pehle Arafat mein wuquf na kar sakne ki wajah se faut ho gaya, aur use baad mein Islam ka Hajj karna hoga.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 874

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A877)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، وَعُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، ابْنَىْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ أَنَّ أَبَاهُمَا عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يُقَدِّمُ أَهْلَهُ وَصِبْيَانَهُ مِنَ الْمُزْدَلِفَةِ إِلَى مِنًى حَتَّى يُصَلُّوا الصُّبْحَ بِمِنًى وَيَرْمُوا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَأْتِيَ النَّاسُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/877" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafj from Salim and Ubaydullah, two sons of Abdullah ibn Umar, that their father Abdullah ibn Umar used to send his family and children from Muzdalifa to Mina ahead of him so that they could pray subh at Mina and throw the stones before everyone (else) arrived</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 877]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/877" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 875

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A878)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 878]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/878" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 878</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   878 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ أَبِي رَبَاحٍ، أَنَّ مَوْلاَةً، لأَسْمَاءَ بِنْتِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ أَخْبَرَتْهُ قَالَتْ، جِئْنَا مَعَ أَسْمَاءَ ابْنَةِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ مِنًى بِغَلَسٍ - قَالَتْ - فَقُلْتُ لَهَا لَقَدْ جِئْنَا مِنًى بِغَلَسٍ ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ قَدْ كُنَّا نَصْنَعُ ذَلِكَ مَعَ مَنْ هُوَ خَيْرٌ مِنْكِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/878" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Asma bint Abi Bakr (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Ata ibn Abi Rabah that a mawla of Asma bint Abi Bakr told him, &quot;We arrived at Mina with Asma bint Abi Bakr at the end of the night, and I said to her, &#x27;We have arrived at Mina at the end of the night,&#x27; and she said, &#x27;We used to do that with one who was better than you</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 878]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/878" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   878 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अस्मा बिन्त आबि बकर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) की आज़ाद लोंड़िया से रिवायत है के हम अस्मा बिन्त आबि बकर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) के साथ मीणा में आये मुँह अँधेरे तोह मैंने कहा के हम मीणा में मुँह अँधेरे आये? अस्मा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) ने कहा: हम ऐसा ही करते थे उस शख्स के साथ जो तुझ से बेहतर थे (रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम). इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) ने सुना बाज़ अहल-इ-इल्म से के वह मकरूह जानते थे कंकरियां मारना क़ब्ल-इ-तुलु-इ-फज्र के नहर के दिन</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/878" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">اسماء بنت ابی بکر کی آزاد لونڈی سے روایت ہے کہ ہم اسما بنت ابی بکر کے ساتھ منی میں آئے منہ اندھیرے تو میں نے کہا کہ ہم منی میں منہ اندھیرے آئے اسماء نے کہا ہم ایسا ہی کرتے تھے اس شخص کے ساتھ جو تجھ سے بہتر تھے ۔ امام مالک نے سنا بعض اہل علم سے مکروہ جانتے تھے کنکریاں مارنا قبل طلوع فجر کے نحر کے دن، اور جس نے ماریں تو نحر اس کو حلال ہو گیا ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/878" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Asma bint Abi Bakr (Radi Allahu Anha) ki aazad londiya se riwayat hai ke hum Asma bint Abi Bakr (Radi Allahu Anha) ke saath Mina mein aaye munh andhere toh maine kaha ke hum Mina mein munh andhere aaye? Asma (Radi Allahu Anha) ne kaha: Hum aisa hi karte thay us shakhs ke saath jo tujh se behtar thay (Rasool Allah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam). Imam Malik (Rehmatullah Alaih) ne suna baaz ahl-e-ilm se ke woh makrooh jaante thay kankariyan maarna qabl-e-tulu-e-fajr ke nahar ke din, aur jis ne maari toh nahar usko halal ho gaya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/878" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
